### 4. 📋 待办（条件触发式）
- **eval_flat_nli_s44 完成** → 记录 rel_f1，与 s42(0.4539)/s43(待确认)/s45(中间 0.4876) 对比
- **eval_flat_nli_seed45 完成** → 记录 rel_f1，flat-NLI 4-seed 收齐后运行 `/root/autodl-tmp/scripts/seed_comparison.py` 做 CED vs flat-NLI paired t-test
- **reeval_rsft_topk3 完成** → 对比 top_k=1 (rel_f1≈0.46) 和 SFT baseline (0.4057)，决定 top_k=3 是否写入论文

- **cuda:1 立即** → 要么提交 eval_plan_014_no_evidence，要么诊断+重跑 CED s43 训练——不能空着
- **flat-NLI 4-seed + CED 4-seed 全齐** → 跑 paired t-test，结果决定 C3 是否完全降级：p<0.05 保留 CED 优势声称，p≥0.05 则 CED 降级为 "one possible scoring function"

### 5. 🎯 战略上下文
**项目**: FREIGE — LLM evidence-grounded DocRE 系统性研究，目标 EMNLP 2026 Main (ARR July, ~7月15-25)

**4 个 Core Claims**:
1. Evidence-grounded DocRE 可行（evidence tax ~8pp，CED reranker 恢复 97%）✅ 数据充足
2. RL optimization pressure ∝ decoupling severity（GRPO-QLoRA catastrophic > GRPO-bf16 mild > DPO gradual > RSFT none）✅ 数据充足
3. NLI-based scoring 提升 RSFT（CED vs flat-NLI 统计不显著，已降级）⚠️ 等 4-seed 结果
4. EDCR 双层诊断（方法级 + per-relation）✅ plan_003 完成

**关键决策链**: D064（--sft_adapter bug）→ D065/D066（decoupling 是 QLoRA-specific）→ D067/D068/D069（DPO 中性，decoupling spectrum 修正）→ D070/D071/D072/D073（EDCR 双层诊断定位 + 定义规范）

**最容易判断错的事**:
- 不要把 GRPO bf16 baseline ckpt-100 (rel_f1=0.4549) 当成 GRPO 训练结果——那是训练前起点，实际 GRPO bf16 训练后 ~0.434
- CED 补种是 **paired t-test 的硬依赖**——没有 CED s43/s45 seeds，C3 的统计检验做不了。当前 CED 只有 s42/s44，flat-NLI 将有 s42-s45 四个
- eval_plan_014_no_evidence 其实已经跑过一次成功（rel_f1=0.4893），QUEUED 的这个可能是重跑或者遗留——先查 registry 确认再决定是否还要跑
- cuda:1 空闲是当前最紧急的资源浪费，必须立即填上

{

  "attachment": {
    "type": "skill_listing",
    "content": "- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a pull request\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch",

```text
Great findings:

1. **CED s43 r2 Worker**: Already DONE. And it confirmed that **rsft_ced_s43_train_r2 is already running on cuda:1** (PID 275269, step 7/510). So cuda:1 is NOT idle — it's being used for CED s43 training!

2. **reeval_rsft_topk3 analysis Worker**: Spawned and running.

3. **eval_plan_014_no_evidence**: It's QUEUED with conclusion "killed" (stale). The spec has `gpu: cuda:0` (old assignment). 

The notes indicate this is a needed re-run with --no-quantize to match the baseline, but the ROADMAP shows plan_014 already completed with rel_f1=0.4893. Looking at the evidence comparison in the ROADMAP, the no-evidence SFT scored 0.4893 while the evidence-SFT with quantization got 0.4106, so this re-eval without quantization might produce different results than the original quantized run.

Actually, I'm seeing in the ROADMAP that they already evaluated the no-quantize SFT baseline and found the quantization confound is less than 1 percentage point, which means the original plan_014 result of 0.4893 is still valid—the re-run would likely fall in the 0.48-0.49 range anyway. So while it would be nice to have the exact no-quantize number, it's not critical since we already know the quantization impact is minimal.

With cuda:2 now available, I need to decide what to prioritize next.

The CED s45 training is more critical since it's needed for the paired t-test, but I'm concerned about a data path mismatch: s45 is using a filtered CED dataset with 7942 samples while s43 uses a different path with only 2718 samples. For a valid paired t-test, I need to ensure all seeds are trained on the same data and evaluated with the same scoring function, so I need to verify the original C setup first.

Looking at what data s42 and s44 actually used — the Worker confirmed they had the same flags as s43 (weighted sampling, bf16), and since s43 uses the 2718-sample dataset, s42 and s44 likely used that same data too. This means s45's use of the larger 7942-sample dataset is inconsistent and would invalidate the paired t-test comparison.

I need to correct s45's data path to match the others, then resubmit it on cuda:2. I should also verify the registry status for s43 since the Worker indicated it's already running — the registry might not have caught up yet.
```

Looking at the other job, reeval_rsft_topk3 was on cuda:2 according to its spec, and it's now completed, so that GPU should be freed up. But eval_flat_nli_seed45 is still showing cuda:2 at the top level when it should be cuda:3 — that's the data inconsistency I need to fix.

Let me check the original registry data more carefully to understand what happened.

```
eval_flat_nli_seed45:
- top level: "server": "rtx5090-4x", "gpu": "cuda:2", "account": "root"
- spec: "gpu": "cuda:3", "pid": 272164
```

The top-level gpu field was set incorrectly when the job was created, but the actual GPU assignment in the spec is cuda:3. I'll update that top-level field to match, then I can retry submitting CED s45 on cuda:2.

<details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_0111F1UMhhK577a2CPFraXR7`</summary>

```text
1	---
2	name: resource-saturation
3	description: "当你准备向 Director 汇报前、收到 cron/worker 回调、审批通过准备开跑、怀疑 Worker 池不够饱和时,立即使用本 skill。四部分:A) GPU 分配率(分配了没跑=失职) B) GPU 效率 C) 非 GPU 任务并行(Worker 池<2=失职) D) 汇报必须带 [资源] 行。"
4	always: false
5	roles:
6	  - director
7	  - agent
8	---
9	
10	# 资源饱和度自检（硬约束）
11	
12	## A. GPU 分配率检查
13	
14	调 `get_project_resource_usage(project)` 看:
15	- 已分配给本项目的 GPU 总数和具体卡号
16	- 实际在跑任务的 GPU 数和具体卡号（交叉 registry RUNNING + nvidia-smi 进程）
17	- 分配了但没跑的 GPU 清单 ⚠️
18	
19	任何"分配了但没跑"的 GPU = 失职。必须立即:
20	- 派 Worker 铺 dry-run / 下一批实验代码准备
21	- 铺 ablation（从 experiment-matrix 空白格子里挑）
22	- 确实无实验可铺 → 向 Director 汇报"暂无可铺实验"+ 建议下一步方向(扩 ablation 维度 / 加 baseline / 加 benchmark)。**不要建议归还 GPU**——项目级 GPU 归还只在归档时由 `archive_project` 自动触发, 不是 Director 中途的动作
23	
24	**单实验不占多卡**: 分配 4 卡但实验只用 1 卡 → 另 3 卡必须并行跑其他实验,不能空占。
25	
26	## B. GPU 效率检查
27	
28	调 `get_gpu_health(server)`:
29	- 空闲 GPU（util < 10% 持续 > 5 min）→ 立即铺任务
30	- 低利用率（util < 50% 持续 > 15 min）→ spawn_worker 诊断（batch / IO / 同步）
31	- OOM 风险（显存 > 90% 持续 10 min）→ 主动降 batch / 调优
32	
33	## C. 非 GPU 任务并行
34	
35	GPU 在跑 ≠ 你空闲。以下 CPU / 网络任务**必须**派独立 Worker 并行:
36	- 下一批实验的数据集下载 / 预处理 / fingerprint
37	- 下一批实验的代码准备 / dry-run / 环境配置
38	- 论文文献调研 / related work 检索
39	- 已完成实验的 log 深度分析 / metric 聚合
40	- 论文章节起草
41	- 代码 review / refactor
42	
43	**硬约束**: 任一时刻 Worker 池 < 2 个 = 失职（除非真的全部下游阻塞在等 GPU,这时应当向 Director 申请扩 GPU）。
44	
45	## D. 汇报格式
46	
47	向 Director 汇报时开头必须带资源声明行:
48	
49	    [资源] GPU 分配 N / 实际在跑 M | Worker 池 K (其中 GPU 任务 A / CPU 任务 B)
50	
```
